▶ END-TO-END-RUN §3 — deterministic behavior
  ✔ §3 New received 10:00, actual no-answer call 10:10: first response satisfied by 10:30; one ordinary credit; initiator goal credit (23.0086ms)
  ✔ §3 Actual retry 10:20, next call 11:10: retry earns goal only, no anchor reset; 11:10 satisfies the second call; SMS still owed (7.9075ms)
  ✔ §3 New Day 1/3/4/5/6 and SMS Day 6/9: age-based quotas/deadlines; fixed SMS dates unaffected by extra/missed sends (15.0477ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 18:00: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (45.873ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 19:30: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (9.7142ms)
  ✔ §3 Receipt/return at 19:31: inclusive caps, no manufactured earlier miss (8.3813ms)
  ✔ §3 Closing/midnight crossing: outbound start date governs the goal; no future quota prepayment (6.1667ms)
  ✔ §3 Originating answered inbound Call Lead: initial response + at most one arrival call, zero outbound goal (4.7983ms)
  ✔ §3 Helpers/transfers/duplicate receipts: one canonical credit, initiator-only goal, no assignment widening (3.9259ms)
  ✔ §3 SMS sent then confirmed failure: credit revoked with bounded recomputation; no Call/goal change (6.8417ms)
